MMISF ACT, 2005.
(1) (2) (3) (4)
Notification-III
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area
of Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act,
--------------
2005 I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer, Bagh
Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia hereby, delineate Area
--------------
of Operation of following WUA’s and direct that the

certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders SCHEDULE—Contd.
and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be displayed on (1) (2) (3)
the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram H. A.
122 0 48 Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division
and Division & at other prominent public places. 123 0 80
127 1 26
Therefore I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer, 128 1 12
Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia also hereby
--------------
declare that no water shall be supplied by the Appropri- Total . . 18 09
ate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of such
--------------
land and that system of supply of water through Water D.O. 181 0 90
User’s Association shall be binding on all the holders R.D. 6350 M. 182/1 1 52
and occupiers of the land under Management of Irrigation 182/2 0 20
System by Farmers. 182/3 0 30
183/1 0 42
Any person affected by this notification or part
183/2 0 80
thereof, may within thirty days from the date of
184/1 0 34
publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, file 184/2 0 30
an appeal before Superitending Engineer, Bhandara 184/3 0 64
Irrigation Circle, Bhandara. 185/1 0 89
185/2 0 40

MMISF ACT, 2005.
294 0 13
NOTIFICATION-III
279 0 09
No. 467-IB-Noti.-3-Bagh Project-2024.— 281 0 27
282 0 06 Whereas, it has been decided to delineate the Area of
276 0 30 Operation of Water User Association (WUAs) on
277 0 14 hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 259 0 12
2005. I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer, Bagh 248 0 11
Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia hereby, delineat 255 0 08
Areas of Operation of following WUAs and direct that the 247 0 75
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders 246 0 40
and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be displayed on the 245 1 31
notice board of the officers, of concerned Gram 188/1 0 20
Panchayat, Tahsil Offices, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division 188/2 0 37
and Division and at other prominent public places ; 250 0 20
256 0 18 Therefore, I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer,
249 0 44 Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia also hereby,
declare that no water shall be supplied by the 251 0 26
Appropriate Authority to an individual holder or occupiers 253 0 67
of such land and that system of supply of water through 257 0 07
Water User Association shall be binding on all the 258 0 09
holders and occupiers of the land under Management of 260 0 13
Irrigation System by Farmers. 262 0 06
263 0 05
Any person affected by this notification or part
261 0 10
thereof, may, within thirty days from the date of
264 0 43
publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, file
265 0 19 an appeal before Superintending Engineer Bhandara
266 0 24 Irrigation Circle, Bhandara.

426 0 15 No. 467-IB-Noti.-3-Bagh Project- 2024.—
427 0 07
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area ———
Total . . 3 40 of Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
———
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
Darvada
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act,
O. L. 2 742 0 12
2005. I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer, Bagh R. D. 1250 M. 743 0 21
744 0 60 Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia hereby, delineate Area
745 0 44
of Operation of following WUA’s and direct that the
747 0 15
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders
748 0 25
and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be displayed on 749/1 0 56
the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram 749/2 0 36
754 0 20
Panchayat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division
753 0 41
and Division & at other prominent public places.
752 0 25
751 0 28 Therefore I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer,
750 0 03 Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia also hereby
755 0 61 declare that no water shall be supplied by the Appropri-
756 1 08 ate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of such
757 0 52 land and that system of supply of water through Water
758 0 46 User’s Association shall be binding on all the holders
759 0 39
and occupiers of the land under Management of Irrigation
760 0 34
System by Farmers.
761 0 29
Any person affected by this notification or part 762 0 22
thereof, may within thirty days from the date of 763 0 34
publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, file 764 0 38
an appeal before Superintending Engineer, Bhandara 766 0 73
Irrigation Circle, Bhandara. 767 0 07

(1) (2) (3) BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
Operation of Water User’s Association (WUA’s) on
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF ACT, 2005.
Irrigation Division, Gondia hereby, delineate Area of
--------------------
operation of following WUA’s and direct that the
BEÚòhÉ . . 325 07 certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders
-------------------- and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be displayed on
the notice board of the offices of concerned Gram
Panchayat, Tahsil Offices, Irrigation Section,
Sub-Division and Division & at other prominent public
places.
Therefore, I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer,
declare that no water shall be supplied by the Appropri-
ate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of such
User Association shall be binding on all the
--------------------
holders and occupiers of the land under Management of
Irrigation System by Farmers.
--------------------
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof,
may within thirty days from the date of publication of this
-------------------- notification in the Official Gazette, file an appeal before
Superintending Engineer Bhandara Irrigation Circle,

No.467-IB-Notification-3-Bagh Project-2024.—
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area
of Operation of Water Users Association (WUA’s)
on hydraulic basis and as per Administrative
-------------- convenience under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3
of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Ex-
-------------- ecutive Engineer, Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation
Operation of following WUA’s and direct that the
holders and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
--------------
displayed on the notice board of the offices of
concerned Gram Panchayat, Tahsil Offices,
--------------
Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and at
other prominent public places;
Therefore, I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive
Engineer, Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia
also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied
-------------- or occupier of such land and that system of supply
of water through Water User’s Association shall be
---------------
binding on all the holders and occupiers of the land
--------------

Any person affected by this notification or part SCHEDULE—Contd.
thereof may, within thirty days from the date of
(1) (2) (3)
publication of this notification in the Official
H. A.

Engineer, Bhandara Irrigation Circle, Bhandara. 487 0 06
